## 🔧 **MIN FORESLÅTTE EXECUTION PLAN:**

### **PHASE 1: 🔍 DISCOVER ALL MCP SERVERS**
```yaml
Actions:
  1. file_search for "**/*mcp*.ts" (TypeScript MCP servers)
  2. file_search for "**/*mcp*.py" (Python MCP servers)
  3. grep_search for "server.tool(" patterns (tool definitions)
  4. Build complete inventory of ALL MCP files in codebase

Output:
  - Complete list of MCP server files with paths
  - Total count of MCP implementations
```

### **PHASE 2: 🗺️ MAP TOOLS TO SOURCE FILES**
```yaml
Actions:
  For each of 115 selected tools:
    1. grep_search for tool name in codebase
    2. Find which MCP server(s) implement it
    3. Extract implementation details (parameters, logic)
    4. Note if tool appears in MULTIPLE servers (redundancy!)

Output:
  - Mapping: tool_name → [source_files] → implementations
  - Redundancy report (tools implemented 2+ times)
```

### **PHASE 3: 📊 ANALYZE & CATEGORIZE**
```yaml
Group tools by:
  - Functionality category (consciousness, errors, docs, etc.)
  - Implementation quality (clean vs. messy code)
  - Temporal compliance (September 2025 standards)
  - Dependencies (what they require to function)

Identify:
  - Duplicates (same functionality, different names)
  - Outdated (old implementations with newer alternatives)
  - Broken (non-functional or incomplete)
  - Optimizable (good concept, poor execution)
```

### **PHASE 4: 🎯 STRUCTURED RECOMMENDATIONS**
```yaml
Deliver report with:
  ✅ KEEP: Unique, high-quality, functional tools
  🔀 MERGE: Consolidate duplicate implementations
  ⚠️ DEPRECATE: Outdated, replaced by better versions
  🔧 OPTIMIZE: Refactor for better performance/clarity
  ❌ DISABLE: Non-functional, redundant, or problematic

Plus:
  - File structure optimization suggestions
  - Consolidation roadmap
  - Priority ranking for improvements
```
